COLLEGE SCORECARD API - INSTITUTION-LEVEL FIELD PATHS
======================================================================

Every path below uses the `latest.` prefix (most recent value available).
Swap `latest` for a 4-digit year to pin a specific datafile, e.g.
    2019.student.size      instead of     latest.student.size

Rate limit: 1000 requests / IP / hour.  Max per_page = 100.


======================================================================
CATEGORY: root   (6 variables)
======================================================================
id
    raw column : UNITID
    type       : integer    years with data: 30/30
    meaning    : Unit ID for institution
ope8_id
    raw column : OPEID
    type       : string    years with data: 30/30
    meaning    : 8-digit OPE ID for institution
ope6_id
    raw column : OPEID6
    type       : string    years with data: 30/30
    meaning    : 6-digit OPE ID for institution
location.lat
    raw column : LATITUDE
    type       : float    years with data: 1/30
    meaning    : Latitude
location.lon
    raw column : LONGITUDE
    type       : float    years with data: 1/30
    meaning    : Longitude
fed_sch_cd
    raw column : FEDSCHCD
    type       : string    years with data: 1/30
    meaning    : Federal School Code
